// Curated symbol names - hand-maintained, preserved across re-scans.
//
// tools/scan_functions.py reads this file when regenerating
// config/symbols.*.txt and uses these names instead of func_XXXXXXXX.
//
// SCOPING: every name sits under a `// target: <name>` header, because the
// same VRAM address holds different code in different builds. A name must
// only be listed under the target whose split actually contains it.
//
// BEFORE ADDING A NAME: check the entry names the function you think it
// does. The scanner will apply any name here to whatever function starts at
// that address, with no further validation - a wrong line silently renames
// an innocent function and, once extracted, spreads into every caller.
//
//   grep -m1 -A6 '^glabel' asm/<target>/nonmatchings/func_<ADDR>.s
//
// For an SDK veneer the address must be a function whose ENTIRE body is the
// three-instruction stub (addiu $t2,$zero,0xA0|0xB0; jr $t2; addiu $t1,$zero,
// <index>). A framed function that happens to CONTAIN a BIOS call is game
// code, not a library member, and must keep its func_XXXXXXXX name - the
// index alone does not identify it. tools/sdk_symbols.py enforces this and
// lists only pure veneers; take names from its report, not from an index you
// spotted by eye.
//
// The trap, live in this tree: the file asm/sces_00269/nonmatchings/
// func_800496C0.s contains a B0/0x09 veneer, so an eye matching on index
// names 0x800496C0 "CloseEvent". That address is a framed function that
// calls func_8003CD28. The veneer is really at 0x80049760, which is a
// different subsegment in the same file. Both the wrong and the right answer
// look plausible from an index alone.
//
// Entries are facts established outside the scan. The SDK veneer names were
// derived with tools/sdk_symbols.py (see config/sdk_symbols.txt for the
// full evidence report).

// Boot small-data flags (docs/TOOLCHAIN.md leading-nop store stub family).
// - boot_flag_0 (word): the only runtime writes measured are 0/1 - cleared
//   by the boot_flag_0 stub (src/func_80024F00, formerly woven-named
//   func_80025AE0; the retail jal encodes the stub at 0x80025AE0 which the
//   carve-view split double-detects inside data_80026524 - adjudicated
//   2026-10-19), jal'd once per iteration by the 10x walk
//   loop at func_800262E4), set to 1 by the twin stub func_80025AF0 (no
//   static caller in the current scan); no static reader anywhere.
// - boot_flag_1 (halfword, element 0 of the halfword array the scanner
//   grouped at its address; retail initial 0x000D): cleared by the stub
//   func_8002A0DC and at func_8002706C; the func_80027100 gate tests ==1,
//   then reads the runtime halfword D_80113DC2 (outside the image = heap)
//   and either branches away or clears the flag and re-enters the walk at
//   func_80026288; the set-site is outside the current scan.

// The memory-card save unit's gap-region buffers (slps_00270 unit
// func_8002ADA0, VA 0x8002ADA0..0x8002B464, decoded cron-202610260919;
// the unit's src references them and no yaml data row hosts them yet -
// the two-pass link defines them in place). All eight carry gp:absolute:
// D_8006974C sits inside slps gp range but retail loads it lui/addiu
// (the _spu_inTransfer precedent), and the D_80115xxx header-block
// bytes/records sit above the range where the curated entry keeps the
// stager's unknown-name rewrite path from ever seeing them. Roles from
// the instruction-level decode: 0x8006974C = the 0x200 work buffer the
// unit fills 0xFF and writes/reads through the card verify path;
// 0x801150F8..FB = the 'SC'+code+sector-count header block (F8 block
// base, F9 'C', FA code byte, FB sector count); 0x80115158 = the 0x30
// name-record copy; 0x801151F8/0x80115278 = the second/third 0x80
// sub-blocks of the save frame (the (short)code==0x13 arm copies
// src6/src7 there, the else arm re-copies src5).
D_8006974C = 0x8006974c; // save unit 0x200 work buffer (0xFF fill, card write/read copy); gp:absolute
D_801150F8 = 0x801150f8; // save unit 'SC' header block base (0x200 save frame); gp:absolute
D_801150F9 = 0x801150f9; // save unit header 'C' byte; gp:absolute
D_801150FA = 0x801150fa; // save unit header code byte; gp:absolute
D_801150FB = 0x801150fb; // save unit header sector-count byte; gp:absolute
D_80115158 = 0x80115158; // save unit 0x30 name-record copy; gp:absolute
D_801151F8 = 0x801151f8; // save frame 0x80 sub-block 2 (src6/src5 arm); gp:absolute
D_80115278 = 0x80115278; // save frame 0x80 sub-block 3 (src7/src5 arm); gp:absolute

// - state_rearm_N: the four-word group the driver re-arms to -1 in one

//
// The shared query-tail entries the reset walker's phase loops call
// (cron-20261012-2026: carved func_80059F40/func_80059FB8 out of
// data_80058DA8; the scan then reclaimed func_80059DB0 above them and
// func_8005A014 below the tail data). func_80059F40 is a 0x18-frame
// block: print-style call func_80037410(a0=D_800C9998), the 42x-jal'd
// func_800598CC, branch on result, then the two mid-stream entries, then
// a clean epilogue (lw ra / jr ra / sp+0x18). Entry contract, fall-through
// values in parens: func_80059F90 stores $v1 (0x12C=300) to u16 counter
// D_800C9F7C via $at=0x800D0000 and bumps $v0; func_80059F9C stores $v0
// (counter D_800CA544 + 1 on fall-through) to u16 D_800CA544 and calls
// func_800527C8 before the shared epilogue. 8 image-wide jal sites enter
// the two entries directly (walker F3 0x8002630C, F4 0x8002640C, phase-2
// loop head 0x80026454, plus 0x80024B28/0x80043570/0x80043678/0x80062C38/
// 0x80062E74; Q1 5x, Q2 3x). None of the six-word prefixes sets $at, so
// whether the entries execute as decoded stays gated on runtime evidence
// - the same gate as the walker's own mid-function entry contract
// (docs/FABRIC.md). EU twin: the same library is already asm on
// mainmenu_eu (scan-carved func_800597F4), entries 0x80059BC0/0x80059BCC
// = JP - 0x3D0.

func_80059F9C = 0x80059F9C; // query tail B: u16 D_800CA544 = $v0, then jal func_800527C8
func_80059FB8 = 0x80059FB8; // die-block: a0=D_80022218 (ptr table + "file not found"), jal func_80037410, u16 D_800C9F7C--

//
// The 588D4 re-bound and the two runtime-opaque blocks (cron-20261012-2141,
// queue item 1 from the previous session). The old data_80058DA8 cut at
// yaml 0x395A8 split a structure, not a boundary. Measured, JP bytes:
//   * func_800588D4's real code runs to 0x80058D90 (row now 0x4bc pinned):
//     the alabel-func_80058D48 tail was rendered .word by the scan but
//     decodes clean - lui+lh to the KNOWN global D_800CA59C, two bne whose
//     targets both land at 0x80058DAC, then write-backs lhu 0x2E(s2) ->
//     D_800C9D0C and lhu 0x30(s2); entered by fall-through from case
//     func_80058D40 (jal func_800518FC, no j-escape) and mid-stream from
//     0x8003bf60/0x8004637c/0x80046384/0x8005f94c/0x80061480 (census).
//   * data_80058D90 0x134 pinned: high-entropy block, 59/77 words
//     plausible-MIPS1 (data control 51/64, code control 59/64), invalid
//     COP2/SDC1 mixes, ZERO image-wide pointer/lui+lo references, and the
//     two bne targets above land INSIDE it (0x80058DAC). Ends with the
//     7-word marker FFFFFF00 FFFFFFFF 00FFFFFF 00185602 00080000 00080000
//     00000000. Byte-identical nowhere in the EU image, no self-copy in
//     JP. Interpretation is runtime-gated (in-place patched/decrypted
//     bytes, or provably-untaken branches into data) - static evidence
//     cannot settle it; same gate family as the $at entries.
//   * func_80058EC4 0x3c pinned: the function's DETACHED TAIL, not a
//     function - epilogue lw ra/s5/s4/s3/s2/s1/s0 +0x48..0x60, jr ra,
//     addiu sp,0x68, the exact inverse of func_800588D4's prologue; first
//     writes sh $v0 -> D_800C9D10 and sh $v1 -> D_800C9D0E (the globals
//     the body reads at 0x80058988), then jal func_8005A514 (mid-function
//     entry inside func_8005A184) with a1 = s5. Reached through the block
//     above or its bne targets; no direct jal site exists.
//   * func_80059520 repeats the pattern one library step up: code
//     [0x80059520..0x800596C4) 0x1a4 pinned with bne 0x800596A0 ->
//     0x800596CC, data_800596C4 0x12c pinned (same 7-word marker tail,
//     4th word 0x02571800), detached tail func_800597F0 0x2c pinned
//     (jal func_80052564 + restores s0-s4/ra sp+0x40, the inverse of
//     func_80059520's prologue).
//   * func_8005981C 0x80 pinned: jr-ra stub + twelve 3-6 word vector
//     negate/swap leaves (lw/negu/jr/sw over a0/a1/a2), each with its own
//     jr ra; the scan's walk ran over them and demoted the row to data
//     before the pin.
// The library between the two patterns is now SPLIT on its measured
// boundaries (cron-20261012-2256). func_80058F08's old 0x618 coarse row
// held 14 functions (heads 80058F08 80058F9C 80058FBC 80059004 80059074
// 8005911C 80059230 800592C8 800593DC 80059468 800594F4 + three 8-byte
// jr-ra stubs) and func_80059A44's 0x4fc coarse row held the clip/cull
// twins 80059A44/80059BC4, stub 80059D4C, leaf 80059D54, and the counter
// state machine 80059DB0 (whose 2026 carve the scan had re-merged). All
// 19 rows are pinned; the per-row sizes sum exactly to the two old rows.
// ENTRY CONTRACT (image-wide census of jal/j, branches, pointer words and
// lui+lo materializations): ZERO static references to any of the 19 row
// starts. The library's only static callers enter at 6 INTERIOR addresses
// (67 jal sites): 0x80058F20 = func_80058F08+0x18 (1x, 0x8003b424;
// counter-bump tail addiu v0,1 / sh v0,0x2c(s0)), 0x800590AC =
// func_80059074+0x38 (16x; indexed u16 read tail lui at,0x800b/addiu
// -0x7b34/addu at,at,v0/lh), 0x80059100 = func_80059074+0x8c (46x;
// clear-fields tail sh zero,0x5e(a1) / sh -1,0x5c(a1) / jr ra / addiu
// sp,0x8 - func_80059074's own frame size, but measured callers carry
// other frames, e.g. func_8005A184's 0x18: the sp convention is
// runtime-gated, same family as the $at entries; two sites 0x8005b748/
// 0x8005c23c are 2-word stubs whose FIRST instruction is the jal),
// 0x800591BC = func_8005911C+0xa0 (1x, 0x8005f4c8), 0x8005936C =
// func_800592C8+0xa4 (1x, 0x8005f4a0), 0x80059410 = func_800593DC+0x34
// (2x, 0x800247c4 + 0x8003bf68). Row starts are reachable only through
// runtime-built control flow - same gate as the state table and the
// runtime-opaque blocks (docs/FABRIC.md). EU cross-check: EU's own scan
// drew data_80058DF4 0x104 / func_80058EF8 0x860 over the same
// neighborhood, so both discs hold this library, but no byte-identical
// blob exists on EU - the JP split is ruled by JP bytes only.
func_80058EC4 = 0x80058EC4; // detached tail of func_800588D4: epilogue sp+0x68, sh v0->D_800C9D10/v1->D_800C9D0E, jal func_8005A514
data_80058D90 = 0x80051800; // runtime-opaque block 1: bne targets 0x80058DAC inside, 7-word marker tail, no static xrefs
func_80059520 = 0x80059520; // clip/cull helper: prologue sp-0x40 s0-s4/ra; bne into block 2 at 0x800596CC
data_800596C4 = 0x800596C4; // runtime-opaque block 2: bne target 0x800596CC inside, same marker tail (0x02571800)
func_800597F0 = 0x800597F0; // detached tail of func_80059520: jal func_80052564, restores s0-s4/ra sp+0x40
func_8005981C = 0x8005981C; // jr-ra stub + twelve vector negate/swap leaves (own jr ra each)
func_800599A8 = 0x800599A8; // jump-table dispatcher: lh 0x80(a0), bounds pass only for state<=4, lw from 0x800221F4+state*4, jr v0; case tails j 0x8005224c. The four in-bounds entries 0x800221F4..0x80022208 are STRING bytes ("t Direct!) (Init Sprite)\n") and the pointer words at 0x80022214+ are out of bounds - the table is runtime-patched or the branch never taken (runtime-gated)
//
// The 19 split rows (cron-20261012-2256), names for the symbols file; the
// entry contract above is the evidence block. Sizes are the pinned rows.
func_80058F08 = 0x80058F08; // library row 0x8c: 0x18 frame; interior counter-bump entry +0x18 (1x)
func_80058F94 = 0x80058F94; // 8-byte leaf: jr ra + sh $zero,0x2c($a0) in the delay slot
func_80058F9C = 0x80058F9C; // library row 0x20: 0x18 frame
func_80058FBC = 0x80058FBC; // library row 0x48: gp-load opener (lui/lw of a 0x800D global) before the 0x18 frame
func_80059004 = 0x80059004; // library row 0x70: no-frame; struct init over a0/a1, ori a3,1 result
func_80059074 = 0x80059074; // library row 0xa0: 0x8 frame; interior entries +0x38 (16x) and +0x8c (46x)
func_80059114 = 0x80059114; // 8-byte leaf: jr ra + nop
func_8005911C = 0x8005911C; // library row 0x114: lw t3,0x10(sp) opener; interior entry +0xa0 (1x)
func_80059230 = 0x80059230; // library row 0x98: 0x30 frame
func_800592C8 = 0x800592C8; // library row 0x10c: 0x20 frame; interior entry +0xa4 (1x)
func_800593D4 = 0x800593D4; // 8-byte leaf: jr ra + nop

func_80059468 = 0x80059468; // library row 0x8c: 0x20 frame
func_800594F4 = 0x800594F4; // library row 0x2c: 0x20 frame; ends at func_80059520's prologue
func_80059A44 = 0x80059A44; // clip/cull twin A 0x180: 0x40 frame (s0-s4/ra), lh fields of an a1-struct, jal func_800521B8

//
// func_8005A014 census (cron-20261012-2256, queue item 3 of 2141): the 5
// image-wide jal sites are real and share one ABI - a0 = 0 (0x800247E4;
// 0x8003AB38's bne-taken path keeps the delay-slot ori a0,3; the
// 0x800634DC/0x80063528 pair) or 3 (0x8003A900), a1 = -1 in the jal
// delay slot (3 sites) or shifted from a loop counter (the 0x800634xx
// pair, flag-gated on bit 0 of u32 0x800CA27C - call skipped when set).
// The body is 18 words of high-entropy noise: no prologue, no jr ra,
// none of the 58Fxx 7-word markers - and NOTHING in the image stores
// into the range (lui+addiu materialization and store census: zero
// hits). Verdict: real callers, opaque body; either the runtime patches
// the bytes before first call through a path with no static writer, or
// the calls are dead as decoded - settle on the pcsx-redux path (now
// unblocked on this host). Same gate family as the 58Fxx blocks; NOT
// carved, the row stays asm with this record.
// RESOLVED cron-20261014-1612 (Queued framing item): the 18 noise words are
// MODE2 sector framing (EDC/ECC + the next sector's sync/address/subheader)
// - tools/deframe.py --framing-rows re-typed the row to pinned data. The
// game de-frames at load (GDB-stub verified, Queued item 2), so the CPU
// never sees these bytes; the 5 jal sites are dead as decoded and the
// emulator leg of the old verdict is moot. The de-framed-payload split
// will re-derive what really executes at 0x8005A014.
data_8005A014 = 0x8005A014; // MODE2 sector framing, was func_ (census above): ABI (a0 device/class id, a1 -1), 18 noise words, zero static writers

// _snd_seq_tick_mode (D_800ABA50): SSINIT public. Role: the effective tick-
// install mode - SsSetTickMode stores (a0 & 0xFFF) when SS_NOTICK else a0,
// the PAL/NTSC arms rewrite it to 0x3C (timer with division) or 5 (the
// VSYNC/RCntCNT3 machinery); the installer dispatches on it and B1C0 divides
// 0x409980 / 0x204CC0 (sysclock/8, sysclock/16 prescale) by it.

// _snd_seq_no_tick (D_800ABA54): SSINIT public. Role: the SS_NOTICK (0x1000)
// bit flag - set by the SsSetTickMode head; gates the installer (bnez ->
// plain return at B1C0/B368) and the uninstaller (plain return with nothing
// undone) - the "SsSeqCalledTbyT is NOT auto-called" state the FUNCTION.TXT
// SS_NOTICK clause describes.
_snd_seq_no_tick = 0x800aba54; // LIBSND SSINIT static (SS_NOTICK flag); evidence under target: g3_eu

// flag), D_800ABA3C (interrupt slot: 6 = RCntCNT2-class timer, 0 = vblank,
// -1 = unregistered), D_800ABA40 (timer users count), D_800ABA44 (PAL/NTSC
// selector: reads == 1 -> the PAL rate 0x32 in cases 0/5 and the RCntCNT3

// stopIntr statics: LIBETC INTR member backing statics for the shutdown

// (INTR.OBJ carries no bss in any sig-db version 2.60-4.70);
// role-descriptive names, evidence-first:
// - intr_active_flag (halfword, INTR state base): stopIntr reads it via
//   la $s0 + lhu 0($s0) at entry and clears it through the same s0 at
//   exit; startIntr/restartIntr share the gate (startIntr sets 1).
//   Equals trap_flag - 2 in every build that curates both.
// - intr_saved_mask (halfword, flag+0x32): stopIntr stores the current
//   mask (read through intr_mask_ptr) before zeroing the port;
//   restartIntr restores it - the cross-member save/restore pair.
// - intr_saved_dicr (word, flag+0x34): stopIntr saves the DMA DICR word
//   (read through intr_dicr_ptr) before the AND 0x77777777 clear;
//   restartIntr restores it.
// - intr_mask_ptr / intr_imask_ptr / intr_dicr_ptr (words, consecutive
//   mask-4 / mask / mask+4): port-pointer slots. stopIntr: mp=mask
//   (save + zero the I_MASK port), ip=imask (stores the re-read zeroed
//   value - the volatile re-read of mp), dp=dicr (AND 0x77777777 =
//   per-channel + master DICR enable clear).
// - intr_stop_begin / intr_stop_commit: the shutdown cluster entries,
//   no-arg jal targets of stopIntr sitting inside the mis-typed data
//   row (real code the scanner types as data; cron-20261013-1241
//   assessment).

// matched body's 44-slot masked word template (opcodes+registers kept,
// address immediates and jal indexes zeroed, branch displacements
// kept) matches ALL EIGHT targets exactly at (unique lui 0x7777 /
// ori 0x7777 pair) - 0x78; the extracted addresses satisfy the layout
// invariants in every target, and mask_ptr equals the trio table.
// Member offsets differ per build (this build: stopIntr at member
// +0x6e0, sces lacks the trio but carries stopIntr) - the bytes, not
// the offsets, identify the member.

// startIntr statics: LIBETC INTR member start-path (member .text+0x198,
// extent 0xdc, ending at trapIntr; sig-db labels loc_210/loc_260 are its
// internal branch targets). No Psy-Q symbol names exist for the statics;
// role-descriptive names, evidence-first:
// - startIntr body (55-slot masked-word fingerprint, g3_eu template,
//   scripts/startintr_fp.py): gate on intr_active_flag (return 0 when
//   set), zero the mask port through intr_mask_ptr, write the re-read
//   zeroed halfword through intr_imask_ptr, store 0x33333333 (the DMA
//   DICR per-channel+master enable pattern) through intr_dicr_ptr,
//   call the start-path aux cluster, derive flag+0x3c into the same s0
//   (array-decay pin overwrite), store flag+0x1018 through it, call
//   intr_restart_begin(flag+0x38) with the arg in the call delay slot,
//   set the gate to 1, run aux4/aux5 (returns stored through
//   intr_start_tab at +0x14/+0x4), intr_restart_commit, and return the
//   flag address (q - 0x3c from the overwritten pin).
// - intr_start_aux1..aux6: the start-path callee cluster (jal slots
//   fixed in the fingerprint; addresses extracted per target).
// - intr_start_tab (word pointer, mask-8): loaded twice by startIntr
//   (the second load after an opaque call - fresh each time); the
//   aux4/aux5 return values are stored at +0x14/+0x4 through it.
// - Fingerprint placement: 5 targets at stopIntr-0x548 (older INTR
//   revision head offset), sces+g3_eu at stopIntr-0x418 (Psy-Q 3.3
//   sig-db offset). g3_jp is SPLIT: head 0x50 bytes at stopIntr-0x548,
//   then a 0x130-byte high-entropy foreign blob spliced mid-function,
//   then the tail (slots 20..54) at +0x130; the member base there
//   (0x80050B08) keeps CheckCallback/GetIntrMask/SetIntrMask and the
//   startIntr head at the sig-db offsets while everything after the
//   splice is shifted +0x130. Retail never calls the INTR subsystem,
//   so the splice shipped unnoticed; g3_jp therefore stays data (no
//   carve, no C unit possible - the function has no contiguous extent).
// - C-attempt status (scripts/startintr/p1.c, probed through cc1 2.6.3):
//   the naive reconstruction diverges - GCC CSE-merges flag+0x38 (the
//   aux2 arg) with restart_begin's arg and hoists it into a callee-
//   saved register (retail computes it in aux2's delay slot from the
//   pin), the pin lands in s1, frame 0x20 vs retail 0x18. The stopIntr
//   shapes carry over but the CSE break + single-register allocation
//   need dedicated probe iterations (next session).
startIntr = 0x8005ECC4; // LIBETC INTR member .text+0x198 (extent 0xdc; 55-slot masked-word fingerprint match, scripts/startintr_fp.py); evidence under target: mainmenu_jp

// restartIntr: LIBETC INTR member (sig-db INTR.OBJ .text+0x660,
// extent 0x88, ending right before memclr). Byte-fingerprint evidence
// (scripts/restartintr_fp.py): the g3_eu body's 34-slot masked word
// template (opcodes+registers kept, address immediates and jal/j
// indexes zeroed, branch displacements kept) matches ALL EIGHT targets
// exactly at stopIntr + delta (0xb0 in 7 builds; mainmenu_jp 0xdb0 -
// its LIBETC build interleaves other members, found by brute force).
// The extracted statics equal the curated intr_* addresses everywhere
// and satisfy saved_mask=flag+0x32, saved_dicr=flag+0x34, dicr=mask+4.
// Semantics: gate on the active flag (nonzero -> return 0), call the
// restart cluster entry with the state + 0x38, restore the saved mask
// halfword through the mask port slot and the saved DMA DICR word
// through the dicr pointer slot, set the flag to 1, call the commit
// entry, then close with the shared-tail jump.
// THE TAIL IS NOT C (probe verdict, scripts/restartintr/, 2026-10-14):
// five source shapes x four opt levels all emit `jal <tail>` + a full
// epilogue - GCC 2.6.3 MIPS has no sibcall. The r6 control shape
// (`return (long)p;`) reproduces the body AND the shared-epilogue
// jump form (`j <local shared epilogue>; move $v0,$s0` with the
// return-0 block falling through) byte-for-byte - only the retail
// jump TARGET differs (an external dispatcher-cluster address). Best
// explanation: a hand-written asm tail in the original SDK source,
// with an ra-clobber bug (the tail block's `jr $ra` returns into
// restartIntr's own jump instruction; dead code in retail, which
// never calls this subsystem). Consequence: restartIntr - and
// trapIntr, which closes with the same form - stay assembly rows.
// intr_restart_begin / intr_restart_commit are the same cluster
// entries startIntr calls (cross-member evidence from the g3_eu
// startIntr body); intr_restart_tail sits mid-body inside the
// dispatcher cluster (the mis-carved mega-region / data rows).
intr_restart_begin = 0x80057588; // INTR restart cluster entry 1, jal from restartIntr with the arg (INTR state + 0x38) set up in the call delay slot; the same entry startIntr calls; evidence under target: mainmenu_jp
intr_restart_commit = 0x80057638; // INTR restart cluster entry 2, no-arg jal from restartIntr after the saved-mask/DICR restore; the same entry startIntr calls; evidence under target: mainmenu_jp
intr_restart_tail = 0x80056F60; // INTR restart shared tail: restartIntr's closing jump target, frame NOT popped and ra left holding the commit call's return address (dead code in retail); a mid-body entry inside the dispatcher cluster; evidence under target: mainmenu_jp
restartIntr = 0x8005F18C; // LIBETC INTR member (extent 0x88; 34-slot masked-word fingerprint match across all 8 targets, scripts/restartintr_fp.py); evidence under target: mainmenu_jp
// intr_trap_flag / intr_mask_ptr: LIBETC INTR.OBJ backing statics
// for the CheckCallback / GetIntrMask / SetIntrMask trio above.
// Role-descriptive names: no Psy-Q symbol name exists - the 3.3
// LIBETC.LIB INTR.OBJ carries no bss and its COFF symbol table
// lists only the member functions plus kernel externals
// (ChangeClearPAD/ResetEntryInt/HookEntryInt/...); every sig-db
// version 2.60-4.70 shows INTR.OBJ bss absent.
// - intr_trap_flag (halfword): CheckCallback returns it (lhu);
//   trapIntr stores 1 on trap entry (ori $v0,1 + sh) and 0 on exit
//   (sh $zero) - the in-trap in-progress flag.
// - intr_mask_ptr (word): the deref'd halfword is the interrupt
//   mask; GetIntrMask returns *(u16*)ptr, SetIntrMask swaps it
//   with $a0 and returns the old value; trapIntr/setIntr/stopIntr/
//   restartIntr/startIntr use it the same way (startIntr stores 0
//   through it).
// Retail placement facts: both sit inside initialized data (the
// flag's initial bytes vary per target - g3_eu flag is the high
// half of the word at 0x80096C08; the g3_eu ptr initial value is
// the tail of the game string "\\DRAGOON\\G33CMXA.STR;1") and no
// EXE code repoints either: the INTR mask/dispatch subsystem is
// linked-in but never called in retail. Addressing: every target
// loads BOTH statics lui+lo ABSOLUTE; slps_00270's copies sit
// ~0x15F8E / 0x14F00 BELOW its gp base 0x8006322C - far outside
// the +/-32K gp_rel window - so the canonical %hi/%lo split is
// exactly retail's lui 0x8005 pair (lo words 0xD29E/0xE32C are
// sign-extended displacements, not positive offsets: the first
// placement pass misread them as 0x8005D29E/0x8005E32C).
// LIBETC.H 3.3: int CheckCallback(void); Get/SetIntrMask postdate
// the 3.3 header (sig-db labels + the bodies give the signatures).
intr_trap_flag = 0x800B3C62; // LIBETC INTR member backing static (halfword); evidence under target: mainmenu_jp
intr_mask_ptr = 0x800B4CF0; // LIBETC INTR member backing static (word pointer); evidence under target: mainmenu_jp
